  P: Open A/P Unpaid Invoices (Reports, then "A/P Unpaid Invoices" under ACCOUNTS PAYABLE in the left menu; the page title reads "A/P Unpaid Invoices Report").
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Choose "Download (CSV)" from the ⋯ menu.
  2. Open the CSV and read its Totals row.
  3. Switch back to the report and read its Totals row.
  4. Scroll down until the next vendors load.
  Step 1:
    - The CSV downloads.
  Step 2:
    - Note the Totals row's Open Balance (it adds up every row of the report).
  Step 3:
    - The screen's Totals row reads the same Open Balance as the CSV's: it adds up every vendor, not only the 30 loaded so far.
  Step 4:
    - The Totals row reads the same after more load.
### DATA-05 | A/R Detail: a long bucket loads in 100s with no line repeated or skipped
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Make sure one aging bucket, bucket A, has between 101 and 400 open lines on today's date.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Detail (Reports, then "A/R Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with every bucket collapsed.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Choose "Download (CSV)" from the ⋯ menu.
  2. Open the CSV and count the rows whose bucket is bucket A.
  3. Expand bucket A.
  4. Scroll to the end of bucket A, letting every batch load, writing down each document number.
  5. Click the "Amount" header.
  6. Look at bucket A and count its lines before any more load.
  7. Scroll to the end of bucket A again, letting every batch load, writing down each number.
  Step 1:
    - The CSV downloads.
  Step 2:
    - Note the count (for example 250).
  Step 3:
    - Bucket A opens and its first 100 lines show.
  Step 4:
    - More lines load as you scroll.
    - Every document appears exactly once.
  Step 5:
    - The rows sort by Amount, largest first, inside each bucket.
  Step 6:
    - Bucket A is still expanded and starts again from its first 100 lines, now in Amount order (largest first).
  Step 7:
    - More lines load as you scroll.
    - In this sort too, every document appears exactly once.
    - Both lists (this one and the one from step 4) hold exactly the CSV's documents for bucket A: none repeated, none missing.

### DATA-07 | A/R Summary: the Totals row adds up exactly the rows each filter leaves
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have open documents in these reports at two locations, location A and location B, with location A's name first in A to Z order.
  P: Make sure you are enrolled at both locations.
  P: Have three customers with only these open documents on today's date:
  P: Customer A: an invoice of $1,000.00 at location A 15 days past due, an invoice of $400.00 at location B 45 days past due and an unused credit memo of $150.00 at location A.
  P: Customer B: an invoice of $700.00 at location A 45 days past due and an invoice of $250.00 at location B 100 days past due.
  P: Customer C: an invoice of $900.00 at location A 5 days past due.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. In the Customer filter, tick customer A and customer B.
  2. In the Location filter, untick location B.
  3. In the Location menu, click "Select all".
  4. In the Past due filter, tick "91+ days".
  5. Choose "Download (PDF)" from the ⋯ menu.
  6. Open the PDF and read its Totals row.
  7. Choose "Download (CSV)" from the ⋯ menu.
  8. Open the CSV and read its Totals row.
  Step 1:
    - Two rows remain.
    - Customer C is gone.
    - Customer A appears once, with Location "Multiple" and a Total of $1,250.00 (1,000.00 + 400.00 − 150.00).
    - Customer B's Total is $950.00 (700.00 + 250.00).
    - The Totals row's Total is $2,200.00 (1,250.00 + 950.00).
  Step 2:
    - The report narrows to location A: customer A's Total is $850.00 (1,000.00 − 150.00, its location A documents only).
    - Customer B's is $700.00.
    - The Totals row's Total is $1,550.00.
  Step 3:
    - Every location is ticked again.
  Step 4:
    - The chip reads "Past due: 91+ days".
    - Only customer B is listed (customer A has nothing more than 90 days past due), at its full balance: 31-60 Days $700.00, 91 And Over $250.00, Total $950.00.
    - The Totals row's Total is $950.00.
  Step 5:
    - The PDF downloads.
  Step 6:
    - The PDF's Totals row reads $950.00 for the Total, the same as the screen.
  Step 7:
    - The CSV downloads.
  Step 8:
    - The CSV's Totals row reads $950.00 for the Total, the same as the screen.

    - The overpaid invoice is not in the row.
  Step 2:
    - The Totals row's Total reads $500.00.
    - Every column of the Totals row equals customer A's row: the Totals row adds up exactly the rows above it.
### DATA-14 | A/R Summary Totals equal A/R Detail's Open Balance, bucket by bucket
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have open documents in these reports at two locations, location A and location B, with location A's name first in A to Z order.
  P: Make sure you are enrolled at both locations.
  P: Have two customers with only these open documents on today's date:
  P: Customer A: an invoice of $1,000.00 at location A that is not yet due, an invoice of $400.00 at location A 45 days past due and an invoice of $300.00 at location B 15 days past due.
  P: Customer B: an invoice of $700.00 at location A 45 days past due, partly paid $200.00 (open $500.00), and an invoice of $250.00 at location A 100 days past due.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with customer A and customer B ticked in the Customer filter and Past due on Any.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Read the Totals row.
  2. Open A/R Detail (Reports, then "A/R Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) from the Reports menu in a second browser tab.
  3. In the Customer filter, tick customer A.
  4. Tick customer B.
  5. In the Location filter, untick location B.
  6. Switch back to the A/R Summary tab.
  7. In the Location filter, untick location B.
  Step 1:
    - The Totals row reads Current $1,000.00, 1-30 Days $300.00, 31-60 Days $900.00 (400.00 + 500.00), 61-90 Days $0.00, 91 And Over $250.00 and Total $2,450.00 (1,000.00 + 300.00 + 900.00 + 250.00).
  Step 2:
    - A/R Detail opens on today's date with no customer ticked and Past due on Any.
  Step 3:
    - Customer A is ticked.
  Step 4:
    - Both are ticked.
    - Each bucket row shows its sums: "91 or more days past due" Open Balance $250.00, "31-60 days past due" Open Balance $900.00 (its Amount reads $1,100.00, the original amounts, which are not compared), "1-30 days past due" $300.00 and "Current" $1,000.00.
    - "61-90 days past due" has no rows.
    - The Totals row's Open Balance reads $2,450.00: bucket by bucket and in total the same as the A/R Summary Totals in step 1.
  Step 5:
    - The report narrows to location A: "1-30 days past due" has no rows any more and the Totals row's Open Balance reads $2,150.00 (2,450.00 − 300.00).
    - The other buckets keep their sums.
  Step 6:
    - A/R Summary shows, as in step 1.
  Step 7:
    - The report narrows to location A: the Totals row reads Current $1,000.00, 1-30 Days $0.00, 31-60 Days $900.00, 61-90 Days $0.00, 91 And Over $250.00 and Total $2,150.00: bucket by bucket the same as A/R Detail in step 5.

    - A/P Summary shows, as in step 1.
  Step 7:
    - The report narrows to location A: the Totals row reads Current $1,000.00, 1-30 Days $0.00, 31-60 Days $900.00, 61-90 Days $0.00, 91 And Over $250.00 and Total $2,150.00: bucket by bucket the same as A/P Detail in step 5.
### DATA-16 | A/R Detail: the Past due ranges split at 30, 60 and 90 days exactly
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have a customer, customer A, with eight open invoices on today's date: one of $10.00 not yet due, and one each 1, 30, 31, 60, 61, 90 and 91 days past its due date, of $1.00, $30.00, $31.00, $60.00, $61.00, $90.00 and $91.00 in that order.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Detail (Reports, then "A/R Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with only customer A ticked in the Customer filter and every group expanded.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. In the Past due filter, tick "1-30 days" only.
  2. Also tick "31-60 days".
  3. Also tick "61-90 days".
  4. Also tick "91+ days".
  5. In the Past due filter, untick all four.
  Step 1:
    - The Totals row reads $31.00: only the invoices 1 and 30 days past due remain (expand the groups to see them).
  Step 2:
    - $122.00: the 31 and 60 day invoices are added ($31.00 + $31.00 + $60.00).
    - The 61 day invoice is not.
  Step 3:
    - $273.00: the 61 and 90 day invoices are added ($122.00 + $61.00 + $90.00).
    - The 91 day invoice is not.
  Step 4:
    - The chip reads "Past due: 4 ranges".
    - The 91 day invoice is added and the invoice that is not yet due is still left out (Totals $364.00 = $273.00 + $91.00).
  Step 5:
    - "Past due: Any" and all eight invoices show (Totals $374.00).

### DATA-18 | A/P Detail: a PDF over 2,000 rows is refused, the CSV downloads
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.

  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/P Detail (Reports, then "A/P Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS PAYABLE in the left menu).
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Choose "Download (PDF)" from the ⋯ menu.
  2. Choose "Download (CSV)" from the ⋯ menu.
  3. Open the CSV and count its document rows (leave out the heading and the Totals row).
  Step 1:
    - The error message "This report is too large for PDF. Export it as CSV, or narrow the filters, then try again." shows straight away: exactly one message, no second generic error.
    - No PDF is downloaded (no new file in the browser's downloads).
  Step 2:
    - The CSV downloads with the "Success" message.
  Step 3:
    - The count matches the report's document rows (for example 13,540).

### DATA-22 | A/R Summary: a payment shows as soon as the report is reloaded
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have a customer, customer A, with one open invoice, invoice A, of $500.00 on today's date, and nothing else open.
  P: Open invoice A's page in a second browser tab, ready to record a payment.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with the Customer filter showing only customer A ticked.

  2. Switch to the second tab.
  3. Record a payment of $200.00 on invoice A.
  4. Switch back to the A/R Summary tab.
  5. Reload the page.
  Step 1:
    - Both read $500.00.
  Step 2:
    - Invoice A's page shows.
  Step 3:
    - The payment is saved and the invoice's balance is $300.00.
  Step 4:
    - A/R Summary shows, not yet reloaded.
  Step 5:
    - The report reloads, still with only customer A ticked, and customer A's Total and the Totals row both read $300.00 (500.00 − 200.00): the payment shows straight away.
### DATA-23 | A/R Summary and Detail Totals differ by what a Past due filter drops
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have two customers with only these open documents on today's date:
  P: Customer A: an invoice of $1,000.00 that is not yet due, an invoice of $400.00 45 days past due, an invoice of $300.00 15 days past due and an unused credit memo of $150.00.
  P: Customer B: an invoice of $500.00 45 days past due and an invoice of $250.00 100 days past due.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide.
  P: Open A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with customer A and customer B ticked in the Customer filter.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. In the Past due filter, tick "31-60 days".
  2. Open A/R Detail (Reports, then "A/R Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) from the Reports menu in a second browser tab.
  3. In the Customer filter, tick customer A.
  4. Tick customer B.
  5. In the Past due filter, tick "31-60 days".
  6. Click the chevron button in the table's top-left header cell (Expand all).
  Step 1:
    - Both customers stay listed (each has an amount 31 to 60 days past due), each with its whole balance.
    - Customer A's Total is $1,550.00 (1,000.00 + 400.00 + 300.00 − 150.00, the not-yet-due amount and the credit memo included).
    - Customer B's Total is $750.00 (500.00 + 250.00).
    - The Totals row's Total reads $2,300.00.
  Step 2:
    - A/R Detail opens on today's date with no customer ticked and Past due on Any.
  Step 3:
    - Customer A is ticked.
  Step 4:
    - Both are ticked.
  Step 5:
    - The chip reads "Past due: 31-60 days" and the report reloads with every group collapsed.
  Step 6:
    - The rows are customer A's $400.00 invoice, customer B's $500.00 invoice and customer A's $150.00 credit memo (every credit is kept).
    - The Totals row's Open Balance reads $750.00 (400.00 + 500.00 − 150.00).
    - It is $1,550.00 less than A/R Summary's $2,300.00 in step 1, exactly what the Detail report leaves out: customer A's $1,000.00 not yet due and $300.00 15 days past due, and customer B's $250.00 100 days past due.

  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have open documents in these reports at two locations, location A and location B, with location A's name first in A to Z order.
  P: Make sure you are enrolled at both locations.
  P: Have a customer, customer A, with a long name (more than 40 characters) and open documents at both locations on today's date, including a credit memo.
  P: Use a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, in light theme at 100% zoom.
  P: Open A/R Collection (Reports, then "A/R Aging Collection" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu).
  P: Leave the report with every group expanded (Expand all).
  P: Open Sales By Customer (Reports, then "Sales By Customer" under PERFORMANCE in the left menu) in a second tab of the same window, for comparison.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Look at the title row.
  2. Look at where the filter row sits, and compare with Sales By Customer.
  3. Read the column headings left to right, letter for letter, capitals included.
  4. Look for a sideways scroll bar under the table.
  5. Look at customer A's rows: the name and Transaction Type cells, and the money, date and number cells.
  6. Look across the whole page for any text that overlaps, is cut off or is clipped.
  Step 1:
    - The title, its info icon directly right of it, and the ⋯ Export button at the right end, all on one line.
  Step 2:
    - The filter row sits below the title row, in the same place as on Sales By Customer.
  Step 3:
    - "Date", "Location", "Transaction Type", "ID", "Due Date", "Past Due", "Amount", "Open Balance" ("Location" shown because two locations are in view; it sits right after "Date").
    - No heading wraps.
  Step 4:
    - There is none: every column is visible at 1440 px without scrolling sideways.
  Step 5:
    - The long name and a long Transaction Type wrap onto a second line.
    - Money, date and number cells stay on one line, and Location does not wrap.
  Step 6:
    - Nothing overlaps, is cut off or clipped.

### D-19 | Design: A/R Summary loading and empty states look as described
  P: Sign in as an Admin user (the "Admin" role), with location A as your current location.
  P: Have open documents in these reports at two locations, location A and location B, with location A's name first in A to Z order.

  P: Open Sales By Customer (Reports, then "Sales By Customer" under PERFORMANCE in the left menu) in a second tab of the same window, for comparison.
  P: If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
  1. Change the as-of date to yesterday and watch the table straight away.
  2. Change the as-of date back to today.
  3. In the Location menu, untick location B.
  4. In the Customer filter, tick customer A.
  5. Read the empty state, letter for letter, and compare it with Sales By Customer's empty state.
  6. Click "Clear all filters".
  Step 1:
    - The native table progress bar shows while the report loads.
  Step 2:
    - The report shows today again.
  Step 3:
    - The report narrows to location A.
  Step 4:
    - The report has no rows: customer A has nothing open at location A.
  Step 5:
    - "No record matches these filters" and "Try changing filters to widen your results.", with a "Clear all filters" button, in the native empty-state look of Sales By Customer.
    - No Totals row.
  Step 6:
    - The filters return to their defaults and rows show.

  Step 2:
    - The report fails to load: "Something went wrong loading this report. Please try again." shows in place of the rows.
    - The standard error message shows as well.
  Step 3:
    - The connection is back.
